Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in dna theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 623 and the invariant I(A,B) = 304 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 623 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dna structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 304. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
